# Configuration inventory and consumer matrix

[Index](README.md) · [CLI knobs](knob_matrix.md)

## Reading this catalog

Every checked-in file under config is cataloged below. Mapping/array containers and scalar leaves retain their exact source paths and line numbers. Array indices identify example records, not a requirement for that exact count. Values are checked-in selections, **not implied code defaults**. Missing scalar keys use consumer-specific defaults or raise validation errors; authoritative defaults/constraints follow below. Passwords, secrets and private material are redacted. Public identifier/path names containing “key” are not private material. File references cite declarations; the consumer column cites implementation.

## Authoritative schemas and precedence

- Source inventories support device lists or mappings, link-driven normalization, managed endpoint flags, platform, mode and pki_profile; default managed is true. SAE IDs are generated from settings. node_a is master and node_b slave, not dynamic election. Links must reference known nodes/interfaces and pass topology validation. Sources: `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:144`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:253`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:316`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:367`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:474`.
- Inventory resolution: existing cwd path, repository-relative path, config/inventory/input/name.yml then .yaml. create normalizes/stores runtime; deploy/bootstrap/validate normally read runtime. Sources: `lib/common/config.py:89`, `qkd_orchestrator.py:1165`, `qkd_orchestrator.py:1470`.
- Required QKD policy: rekey_enabled, key_batch_size, max_installed_keys. Ring even 4..64; batch 1..ring; execution >=1; activation positive; floor >=ACK; grace must fit two-activation-minus-execution horizon; RPC rotation 0 or >=60. --interval targets activation, not execution cadence. Sources: `lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:371`, `lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:480`.
- BGP/LDP root/session schema, algorithms, IDs and endpoints: [AO profile analysis](tcp_ao_orchestrators.md). Sources: `lib/qkd/bgp_tcp_ao.py:18`, `lib/qkd/ldp_tcp_ao.py:18`.
- KME YAML is loaded as a mapping, with strict required lookups at individual modules rather than a global schema validator. Count precedence is explicit flag then configured runtime derivation (otherwise error); create/deploy/build-env default flag count 2. Sources: `lib/kme/state.py:29`, `lib/kme/state.py:72`, `lib/kme/compose.py:109`, `kme_orchestrator.py:331`.
- The upstream repo URL is spelled cybermerqury in checked-in YAML; “cybermercury” in the requested goals refers here to that configured repository. Linux code clones/builds it; this analysis does not inspect upstream behavior. Sources: `config/kme/lab.yaml:21`, `lib/kme/build_env.py:254`, `lib/kme/build_image.py:494`.
- Password environment precedence and all QKD account paths are in [QKD lifecycle](qkd_orchestrator.md); declared class can be super-user and SSH host checking can be disabled in lab inputs. Sources: `lib/common/script_user_bootstrap.py:139`, `config/inventory/inventory_base.yaml:7`, `config/kme/lab.yaml:13`.

## Generated runtime configuration (not checked in here)

The config/runtime directory was absent in this worktree during analysis; the contract below comes from writers, not observations of deployed routers. Sources: `lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:506`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:547`, `lib/qkd/onbox_builder.py:354`.

| Generated object | Schema / purpose | Writer → consumers |
|---|---|---|
| topology.yaml | normalized nodes mapping and links list; endpoint managed/IP/KME/platform/identity and explicit CA/keychain | `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:435`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:547` → create/build artifacts/observers |
| devices.yaml | device mapping; hostname/ip/platform/managed/mode/qkd.sae_id/kme and links with role/peer/iface/CA/keychain | `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:474`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:515` → bootstrap/deploy/clean/PKI/AO/KME count |
| pki_profile.yaml | selected profile and associated path information | `lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:311` → cert builders and provisioning |
| qkd_policy.yaml | validated policy plus CLI overrides | `lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:452` → deploy/onbox builder |
| per-device qkd_onbox_config.json | policy, runtime defaults, cert/trust, user/path/log/RPC/BGP/LDP settings | `lib/qkd/onbox_builder.py:232` → onbox config validation/load at `artifacts/qkd_onbox.py:116` |
| per-device qkd_onbox_inventory.json | device_name,hostname,local_sae,kme_ip,kme_port,links array | `lib/qkd/onbox_builder.py:299` → onbox per-device runtime |
| signatures/PKI markers | reuse identity; not complete policy/cert-content hashes | `qkd_orchestrator.py:113`, `qkd_orchestrator.py:1325` → create reuse decision |

## Input-file distinctions and inert metadata

The _orig/lab.orig files are alternatives, not automatically merged backups. File selection is explicit/default-path based. lab_evo still declares an Ubuntu Linux host; its filename does not implement Junos EVO container deployment. SRX is a platform label/cipher file, not IPsec VPN logic. Sources: `config/kme/lab_evo.yaml:1`, `config/inventory/platforms/srx.yaml:1`, `lib/common/config.py:89`, `kme_orchestrator.py:326`.

No Kubernetes install schema or IPsec/SRX VPN quantum-slot schema is wired into the enumerated orchestrator parsers/config builders. This is an absence finding bounded to these entry points, not a claim about every file in the repository. Sources: `qkd_orchestrator.py:252`, `kme_orchestrator.py:354`, `lib/qkd/onbox_builder.py:232`.

Catalog coverage: **42 checked-in config files; 1519 YAML mapping/array/scalar rows**. Template expression rows and generated contracts are additional.

## Missing-field defaults, aliases and validation (source schema contract)

| Input path | Type / default when absent | Active consumers and constraints |
|---|---|---|
| devices | list or mapping; required | create normalization errors on missing/unsupported format; mapping keys are names, list records need name or hostname (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:144`) |
| devices[].name | string, falls back hostname | unique names required (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:170`) |
| devices[].platform | string, source inventory platform fallback, else empty | lowercase; mixed inventory should specify each device (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:180`) |
| devices[].managed | boolean, true | controls runtime target/build inclusion (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:181`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:515`) |
| devices[].topology_member | boolean, true | legacy metadata only; does not generate links (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:183`) |
| devices[].ip / mgmt_ip | string; mgmt_ip alias if ip absent | controller transport; normalize to string (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:187`) |
| devices[].kme | mapping or IP string; empty mapping if absent | nested ip string and port int; sync flattened kme_ip/kme_port (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:192`) |
| devices[].interfaces | array of strings; [] | linked interfaces may be added by normalized topology; nonarray errors (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:216`) |
| devices[].auth | mapping; copied default_auth only if absent | resolved credentials available to transport (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:221`) |
| devices[].qkd.sae_id / sae_id | string; generated sequential SAE from settings | explicit nested first, flattened fallback, then generated (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:224`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:86`) |
| links | array; [] | explicit links, no automatic full mesh; nonarray errors (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:253`) |
| links[].node_a,node_b | strings; required; aliases a_node/a,b_node/b | require paired interfaces (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:267`) |
| links[].interface_a,interface_b | strings; required; aliases a_if/if_a,b_if/if_b | malformed link errors (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:269`) |
| links[].type | string, link; macsec normalized to link | link metadata (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:281`) |
| links[].macsec | boolean, true | explicit feature linkage (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:284`) |
| links[].managed_a,managed_b | boolean, default referenced node managed; unknown node false | managed endpoint normalization (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:288`) |
| links[].id | string, node_a-node_b | derived if absent (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:298`) |
| links[].ca_name / keychain_name | string; sanitized deterministic derived names | explicit names retained by name helpers (`lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:238`, `lib/qkd/topology_builder.py:247`) |
| qkd_policy.batch_enabled | boolean, true | setdefault after overrides; other supplied policy preserved (`lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:486`) |
| qkd_policy.interval_seconds | integer alias | when activation_interval field exists, CLI interval overwrites activation field; does not change execution interval (`lib/qkd/inventory_builder.py:480`) |
| KME required nested fields | missing mapping/key raises via require | no universal merge of lab.yaml for a partial --config file; command-specific require lookups (`lib/kme/state.py:72`) |
| KME count | integer flag, public default 2; modules may pass None | None requires enabled runtime derivation and nonzero count; disabled derivation errors, no silent count fallback (`lib/kme/compose.py:109`, `kme_orchestrator.py:331`) |

## Hardcoded shared settings (not YAML input)

These dictionaries are code-level defaults, not environment variables or YAML flags. Runtime identity resolution can override account/path values, and presence of a setting is not proof every consumer enforces it. Sources: `lib/common/settings.py:1`, `lib/common/script_user_bootstrap.py:139`, `lib/qkd/onbox_builder.py:232`.

| Setting | Code default | Primary consumer / boundary | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
